function detect() {
  const h = window.location.hostname
  if (h.includes('glasskiller'))                          return SITES.glasskiller
  if (h.includes('xn--12cl1c7') || h.includes('coaster')) return SITES.coaster   // à¸à¸µà¹à¸£à¸­à¸à¹à¸à¹à¸§.com
  if (h.includes('xn--s3cmc7d') || h.includes('wood'))    return SITES.wood      // à¸à¹à¸²à¸¢à¹à¸¡à¹.com
  if (h.includes('xn--22c6bed') || h.includes('baan'))    return SITES.baan      // à¸à¹à¸²à¸à¹à¸¥à¸à¸à¸µà¹.com
  return SITES.printsticker // default
}

function apply(s) {
  document.documentElement.lang = s.lang
  document.title = s.name + ' â Coming Soon'

  // CSS vars
  const r = document.documentElement.style
  r.setProperty('--c1', s.c1)
  r.setProperty('--c2', s.c2)
  r.setProperty('--c1-light', s.cLight)
  r.setProperty('--glow', s.glow)

  document.getElementById('bg').style.background = s.bg
  document.getElementById('b1').style.background = s.c1
  document.getElementById('b2').style.background = s.c2
  document.getElementById('b3').style.background = s.c1

  document.getElementById('icon').textContent       = s.icon
  document.getElementById('brandName').textContent  = s.name
  document.getElementById('badgeText').textContent  = s.badge
  document.getElementById('line1').textContent      = s.line1
  document.getElementById('line2').textContent      = s.line2
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent        = s.sub
  document.getElementById('formLabel').textContent  = s.formLabel
  document.getElementById('notifyBtn').textContent  = s.btn
  document.getElementById('success-msg').textContent = s.success
  document.getElementById('footer').innerHTML       = s.footer

  // Pills
  const pc = document.getElementById('pills')
  pc.innerHTML = s.pills.map(([ic,tx]) =>
    `<div class="pill"><span>${ic}</span>${tx}</div>`).join('')

  // Countdown labels
  const ids = ['lblDays','lblHours','lblMins','lblSecs']
  s.lbls.forEach((l,i) => document.getElementById(ids[i]).textContent = l)

  // Email placeholder
  document.getElementById('email-input').placeholder = s.lang === 'th' ? 'à¸­à¸µà¹à¸¡à¸¥à¸à¸­à¸à¸à¸¸à¸' : 'your@email.com'
}

// ââ Init ââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââ
const site = detect()
apply(site)

// ââ Countdown â Sep 1 2027 âââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââ
const LAUNCH = new Date('2027-01-01T00:00:00+07:00')
function tick() {
  const diff = Math.max(0, LAUNCH - new Date())
  const pad  = n => String(Math.floor(n)).padStart(2,'0')
  document.getElementById('days').textContent  = pad(diff / 86400000)
  document.getElementById('hours').textContent = pad((diff % 86400000) / 3600000)
  document.getElementById('mins').textContent  = pad((diff % 3600000) / 60000)
  document.getElementById('secs').textContent  = pad((diff % 60000) / 1000)
}
tick(); setInterval(tick, 1000)

// ââ Notify ââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââââ
function handleNotify() {
  const v = document.getElementById('email-input').value
  if (!v || !v.includes('@')) return
  document.querySelector('.form-row').style.display = 'none'
  document.getElementById('success-msg').style.display = 'block'
}
document.getElementById('email-input').addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.key === 'Enter') handleNotify()
})
